Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The laws variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 742 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 393 and intersection form of rank 1.

This completes the proof. QED.
